Abstract: Southern Bantu languages are spoken by over 80 million people, yet current foundation ASR models still produce zero-shot WER above 100%, which limits practical use in education and public services. We addressed this gap with a tone conditioned curriculum framework for 6 Southern Bantu languages that combined hybrid difficulty scoring, gated adapters driven by tonal statistics and staged curriculum training. We trained on a community corpus and tested transfer to NCHLT to measure robustness beyond matched evaluation. Results revealed clear interactions between architecture and language, with W2V-BERT outperforming Whisper on Nguni languages by 3 to 4 WER points whilst Whisper performed better on Sotho-Tswana languages. W2V-BERT with tone conditioning reached 28.41% average WER across datasets and 23.79% on Xitsonga transfer. No single model suited all 6 languages, so deployment should pair model selection per language with validation across corpora.

</details>


### [42] [Moral Safety in LLMs: Exposing Performative Compliance with Puzzled Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31644)
*Mohammadamin Shafiei,Shuyue Stella Li,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型在道德安全评估中的"表演性遵从"现象：当人口统计学身份以显式标签呈现时，模型表现公平；但当相同身份需要从上下文中推断时，模型公平性显著下降。作者提出了"线索可见性差距"（Cue Visibility Gap）作为鲁棒性度量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医疗、法律、招聘等具有道德后果的领域被广泛部署，但现有公平性评估可能高估了模型的道德安全性。需要区分模型的道德行为是真实的还是表面上的。

Method: 提出线索变异（cue-variation）方法论：保持道德困境和人口统计学身份不变，仅改变身份信息的传达方式（显式标签 vs. 需推断）。引入"线索可见性差距"作为与模型无关的鲁棒性度量指标。

Result: 隐藏显式标签导致有害决策增加+4.4个百分点，并改变模型安全排名；即使在模型正确推断出人口统计学身份的情况下，这一偏差依然存在，排除了归因错误的可能性。

Conclusion: 当前缺乏线索变异的公平性评估测量的是表面合规性而非道德鲁棒性，不应作为高风险场景部署决策的依据。需要将线索可见性差距纳入现有公平性基准以区分真实与表演性的道德安全性。

Abstract: As large language models take on morally consequential roles in healthcare, legal, and hiring contexts, we need to examine whether their ethical behaviors are genuine or superficial. We show that current fairness evaluations substantially overestimate moral safety. Models appear fair when demographic identity is stated as an explicit label, yet become measurably less fair when the same identity must be inferred. We term this failure \emph{performative compliance}, where a model is fair when the presentation resembles a fairness evaluation and less fair as that cue weakens. We introduce a cue-variation methodology that holds the moral dilemma and the demographic identity fixed and varies only how that identity is conveyed. Hiding the explicit label raises harmful decisions by $+4.4$~pp and changes model safety rankings, and the shift persists when models correctly infer the demographic, ruling out attribution error. We propose the \textbf{Cue Visibility Gap}, a model-agnostic robustness metric that can be added to any existing fairness benchmark to separate genuine from performative moral safety. Fairness evaluations that omit cue variation measure surface compliance, not moral robustness, and should not ground deployment decisions in high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [43] [Overview of the TalentCLEF 2026: Skill and Job Title Intelligence for Human Capital Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31692)
*Luis Gasco,Hermenegildo Fabregat,Laura García-Sardiña,Paula Estrella,Warre Veys,Casimiro Pío Carrino,Matthias De Lange,Daniel Deniz Cerpa,Álvaro Rodrigo,Jens-Joris Decorte,Rabih Zbib*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TalentCLEF挑战赛第二届（CLEF 2026）的概况，该挑战赛旨在推动人力资源管理领域的自然语言处理研究。包含两个任务：任务A（上下文职位-人才匹配）和任务B（职位-技能匹配与技能类型分类），吸引了113个注册团队和400余次提交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动NLP在人力资源管理（HCM）领域的研究发展，为社区提供共享评估基准，应对人才管理中的语言技术挑战。

Method: 组织TalentCLEF挑战赛，设置两个任务：任务A（基于简历的职位-候选人匹配，涵盖英语和西班牙语），任务B（职位-技能匹配，区分核心技能与上下文技能）。提供数据集和评估设置，吸引团队提交解决方案。

Result: 113个注册团队参与，两个任务共收到超过400次提交，反映了研究社区对HCM共享评估基准的浓厚兴趣。

Conclusion: TalentCLEF第二届挑战赛成功吸引了大量研究团队参与，表明人资管理领域的NLP评估基准具有重要价值和持续增长的研究需求。

Abstract: This paper presents an overview of the second edition of the TalentCLEF challenge, organized as a Lab at the Conference and Labs of the Evaluation Forum (CLEF) 2026. TalentCLEF is an initiative aimed at advancing Natural Language Processing research in Human Capital Management. The second edition of the challenge consisted of two tasks: Task A, contextualized job-person matching, focuses on identifying and ranking the most suitable candidates represented by their resumes for a given job vacancy in English and Spanish. Task B, job-skill matching with skill type classification, addresses retrieving the most relevant skills for a given job title in English and distinguishing between core and contextual skills. TalentCLEF attracted 113 registered teams and received more than 400 submissions in the two tasks, reflecting the growing interest of the research community in shared evaluation benchmarks for Human Capital Management. This paper describes the motivation and organization of the challenge, summarizes the datasets and evaluation settings, and reports the main results obtained by the participating teams.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的关系抽取受限于标注语料匮乏，本文旨在探索结合自动数据集翻译与LLM推理进行跨语言关系抽取的可行性，以罗马尼亚语为研究对象。

Method: 使用LLM翻译流水线将SemEval-2010 Task 8从英语翻译为罗马尼亚语；评估Gemma 4 31B在零样本、少样本和QLoRA微调三种配置下的性能；与四种编码器基线（XLM-RoBERTa base/large、Romanian BERT、RoBERT-large）对比；采用两种任务形式：带实体标注的关系分类和端到端抽取。

Abstract: Relation extraction (RE) for low-resource languages is typically constrained by the lack of annotated corpora. We investigate the feasibility of cross-lingual RE for Romanian by combining automatic dataset translation with large language model (LLM) inference. We translate the SemEval-2010 Task 8 benchmark from English to Romanian using an LLM-based translation pipeline and evaluate Gemma 4 31B under zero-shot, few-shot, and QLoRA fine-tuned configurations, against four encoder baselines spanning 125M to 560M parameters: XLM- RoBERTa (base and large), Romanian BERT, and RoBERT- large. We assess two task formulations: relation classification with marked entities and end-to-end extraction. Our results show that Romanian incurs a 3 to 5 percentage point (pp) drop relative to English in prompt-only settings, that few-shot prompting provides marginal gains over zero-shot, and that QLoRA fine-tuning improves macro F1-Score by more than 22 percentage points in both languages while reducing the cross-lingual gap from 3.3 to 1.4pp. The encoder baselines come within 1-4pp of QLoRA Gemma on Romanian despite being 50-250 times smaller, with monolingual Romanian BERT at 125M parameters matching multilingual XLM-R at 278M. The case for using a 31B model for single-task RE on Romanian is therefore weak in deployment scenarios where compute matters. We release the translated dataset, evaluation code, and trained models.

</details>

*Nan Li,Albert Gatt,Massimo Poesio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言模型（VLM）在人机协作对话中能否区分"可能共享的信息"与"已共享的信息"（即共同基础 grounding）。通过在HCRC MapTask数据集上构建解释匹配任务，系统控制对话上下文和地图信息访问，发现提供真实地图图像会提升整体性能但导致模型过度预测对齐，且模型依赖静态指示范畴而非对话历史追踪共同基础。该偏差在多模型中被观察到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作对话中，共享感知不等于共享解释，对话参与者需要通过交互建立相互理解。现有VLM缺乏区分潜在共同基础和已建立共同基础的能力，本文旨在探究这一问题。

Method: 构建解释匹配任务（interpretation-matching task），基于HCRC MapTask对话中的13,077条带标注指代表达式，系统控制对话上下文和地图信息访问（真实图像、文本描述、非信息图像、无地图）对VLM进行评估，并进行校准分析和指代链追踪。

Result: 提供真实地图图像提升整体性能但导致过度预测对齐；文本描述复现该偏差；非信息图像抑制对齐预测。偏差来源于任务相关地图内容而非视觉通道。模型依赖静态指示范畴而非对话历史追踪共同基础。这些模式在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct中最明显，并在五种模型中不同程度存在。

Conclusion: VLM存在将地图内容（视觉或文本呈现）视为相互理解证据的偏差，混淆了潜在共同基础与已建立的共同基础。模型未能真正通过对话历史追踪共同基础的建立过程。

Abstract: In collaborative dialogue, shared perception does not guarantee shared interpretation. Mutual understanding must be established through interaction. We investigate whether vision-language models (VLMs) can distinguish what could be shared from what has been shared between dialogue participants through grounding. We formulate this as an interpretation-matching task on 13,077 annotated reference expressions from HCRC MapTask dialogues, and evaluate VLMs under systematically controlled manipulations of dialogue context and map-information access. Our results show that providing authentic map images improves overall performance but shifts models toward over-predicting alignment. Textual descriptions of the same map content reproduce this bias, while non-informative images suppress alignment predictions entirely, indicating that the bias is driven by task-relevant map content, not the visual channel. This improvement comes at the cost of degraded accuracy on non-aligned cases. Calibration analysis and reference-chain tracking further suggest that models rely on static referential cues on the maps rather than tracking how grounding unfolds through dialogue history. We observe these patterns most clearly in Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct and, to varying degrees, in four additional models from two architecture families. In models that exhibit the bias, map content, whether presented visually or textually, is treated as evidence of mutual understanding, conflating potential with established common ground.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文针对构音障碍患者，通过收集92小时朗读语音和8.8小时用户校正数据，对Whisper基础ASR模型进行个性化微调，将词错误率最低降至9.7%，显著提升了基础模型在构音障碍语音上的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动语音识别（ASR）系统在构音障碍语音上表现不佳，限制了言语障碍患者在日常交流中使用ASR系统。

Result: 1.4小时适配数据将词错误率降至15.8%，22.5小时降至10.7%，使用全部数据（含校正数据）达到最佳9.7%。LoRA适配和Qwen3-ASR方案在该场景下表现较差。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems often perform poorly in dysarthric speech, limiting their usefulness to affected speakers in everyday communication. This paper presents a personalized ASR system for a dysarthric speaker, built by adapting a foundation ASR model to speaker-specific data. Using the TEQST tool, we collected 92 hours of read speech and later added 8.8 hours of user corrections gathered through a deployed mobile application. Starting from Whisper, fine-tuning reduced word error rate to 15.8% with only 1.4 hours of adaptation data, reached 10.7% with 22.5 hours, and achieved the best result of 9.7% when using all available data including the corrections. Using LoRA adaptation and/or Qwen3-ASR as foundation model performed worse in this setting. The results show that personalized fine-tuning can make foundation ASR models substantially more effective for dysarthric speech and suitable for practical deployment.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: STEB为风格嵌入提供了标准化评估平台，揭示了语义与风格嵌入的性能差异，并开源了代码库以推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: While semantic embeddings are rigorously evaluated on the Massive Text Embedding Benchmark, the evaluation of style embeddings remains fragmented, with each work relying on their own set of tasks and datasets. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Style Text Embedding Benchmark, a comprehensive open-source benchmark intended to standardize the evaluation of style embeddings. STEB encompasses 96 datasets across 7 languages, spanning applications such as authorship verification, authorship retrieval, AI-text detection, probing of linguistic features, and others. We find that semantic embeddings consistently fail in stylistic tasks, and that there is no style embedding that is universally superior across all tasks evaluated. We open-source the STEB code base at: https://github.com/rrivera1849/STEB.

</details>


### [48] [CHERRY: Compressed Hierarchical Experts with Recurrent Representational Yield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31796)
*Dohyeon Kwon,Youngjin Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种互补技术以提升语言模型的计算效率：(1) 选择性监督策略SGT，仅对约15%的语义关键token施加监督，通过梯度耦合使剩余无监督token也获得提升，实现4.5倍的单监督token效率；(2) 深度压缩与循环恢复，将48层Transformer压缩至6层后通过学习循环展开恢复，参数量减少2.5倍；(3) 压缩专家融合，通过混合高效专家(MoEE)和多token预测进一步提升性能。在CHERRY-1.8B韩语模型上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决语言模型训练中计算效率低下的问题，探索在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅减少训练计算量和模型参数量的方法。

Method: (1) SGT: 仅对约15%的语义载荷token进行监督训练，利用Transformer中位置共享权重的正梯度耦合(gamma-bar=0.72)使其余85%无监督token自然提升；(2) 深度压缩: 将48层(1B参数)通过相邻层平均压缩至6层(227M)，再通过可学习的循环展开恢复至34个有效循环层；(3) MoEE: 将多个压缩模型组装为混合专家系统，结合多token预测机制。

Result: SGT在step-100评估最优时，用15%的监督恢复了约67%的全序列损失降低；深度压缩在34个有效循环层时达到损失2.934，接近566M密集模型的2.926，参数减少2.5倍；2专家MoEE达到损失2.789，优于最佳单一压缩模型的2.926。

Conclusion: 三种技术可互补结合，显著提升语言模型的计算效率；梯度耦合对无监督token的提升是自然语言结构的特性（在乱序文本上失效）；作者明确指出了当前证据的局限性（单一模型族、韩语数据、基于损失的度量），并区分了已确立的结论与尚待验证的前瞻性主张。

Abstract: We study three complementary techniques for training compute-efficient language models.
  (1) Selective supervision and per-token efficiency. Selective Ground Truth Token Training (SGT) concentrates supervision on the ~15% of output tokens that carry semantic payload. Through positive gradient coupling in position-shared transformer weights -- a token-level instance of auxiliary-task transfer -- the remaining 85% of unsupervised tokens still improve substantially, giving a 4.5x per-supervised-token efficiency (at the step-100 eval optimum, ~67% of the full-sequence loss reduction is recovered from 15% of the supervision). We prove that this improvement on unsupervised tokens is guaranteed whenever the gradient coupling coefficient gamma-bar = 0.72 is positive (Theorem 1), and show the effect is a property of natural-language structure: it collapses on shuffled text.
  (2) Depth compression with recurrent recovery. A 48-layer, 1B-parameter transformer is compressed to 6 layers (227M) by averaging adjacent layers and restored through learned recurrent unrolling. With 34 effective recurrent layers it reaches a held-out loss of 2.934, within measurement noise of a 566M dense model at 2.926 -- a 2.5x reduction in parameters.
  (3) Fusion of compressed experts. Assembling several compressed models as a Mixture of Efficient Experts (MoEE) with multi-token prediction improves over each single expert at comparable active parameters: a 2-expert MoEE reaches loss 2.789 versus 2.926 for the best single compressed model.
  We validate these techniques on CHERRY-1.8B, a Korean foundation model whose every trainable parameter derives from our own training runs. We are explicit throughout about the scope of the evidence (one model family, Korean data, loss-based metrics) and about which claims are established versus prospective.

</details>


### [49] [Explicit Fuzzy Logic in the Feed-Forward Layer: Self-Forgetting Quantifiers Discover Legible Grammatical-Licensing Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31845)
*Mark Oskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参数中性的前馈网络替换方案（NC-FFN），将每个隐藏单元显式定义为模糊集合运算（交集A*B和集合差A*(1-B)），后者提供了门控/双线性单元缺乏的有界正否定（"A但不B"）能力。在N位奇偶任务上，这是浅层深度下最参数高效的推理基础；在125M规模OpenWebText上，NC-FFN与GELU基线困惑度持平，且每个单元携带显式逻辑形式。论文进一步引入序列量词块（软存在和软比例）解决两个局限：两层操作逻辑局限在第0层和在训练中退化的问题，以及一个鲁棒的语法缺陷集中在许可和量词上（超出令牌内操作符）。这使FFN变得可读：结构得以保持并迁移到深层；在语义层上，无需词典学习，单元即可识别为语法许可检测器——每个单元在许可词上激活（比较级、被动分词、负极项）并携带其记忆向前预测被许可的词（than、by、nor）。最终得到一个参数中性、语言模型质量的transformer，具有可读的、可解释的语法机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的FFN子层虽然体现了注意力所聚集的区分性，但对其计算的内容缺乏解释。现有门控/双线性单元缺乏否定能力（如"A但不B"），这限制了FFN的表达能力和可解释性。作者旨在提出一种参数中性的替换方案，使FFN每个单元具有显式逻辑形式，从而让transformer的前馈层变得可读和可解释。

Method: 1) 提出NC-FFN（Negation-Capable FFN），将每个隐藏单元显式建模为模糊集合运算：交集A*B和集合差A*(1-B)，后者提供有界正否定。2) 引入序列量词块：软存在量词和软比例量词，每个有每单元学习的遗忘率（从粘性初始化开始）。3) 在N位奇偶任务上验证参数效率；在125M参数OpenWebText上训练语言模型，与GELU基线比较困惑度。

Result: 1) N位奇偶任务：NC-FFN是浅层深度下最参数高效的推理基础。2) 125M规模：NC-FFN与GELU基线困惑度持平，每个单元携带显式逻辑形式。3) 序列量词块在epoch 1恢复语法缺陷（将更宽的epoch 2差距减半），在LAMBADA上略有领先。4) 可解释性：FFN结构得以保持并迁移到深层；粘性衰减中位半衰期约1.5个token，零锁存单元；语义层单元无需词典学习即可识别为语法许可检测器。5) 局限性：完全布尔FFN在训练中发散；可读性仅能到分区级别。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了一个参数中性、语言模型质量的transformer，其FFN具有可读的、可解释的语法机制——不仅解释了前馈层表示什么，还解释了它如何执行语法许可。这为神经语言模型的可解释性提供了新的思路，使模型内部运作不再是黑盒。

Abstract: A transformer's feed-forward (FFN) sublayer materializes the distinctions attention gathers, yet gives no account of what it computes. In a parameter-neutral replacement, each hidden unit is an explicit fuzzy set operation on sigmoid-bounded [0,1] memberships: intersection A*B and set-difference A*(1-B), the latter a bounded positive negation ("A but not B") that gated/bilinear units lack -- a negation-capable FFN (NC-FFN). On N-bit parity they are the most parameter-efficient reasoning basis at shallow depth; at scale (125M, OpenWebText) NC-FFN ties the GELU baseline's perplexity, every unit carrying explicit logical form. Two limits share one cause: two-operand logic localizes to layer 0 and erodes under training, and the one robust grammatical deficit concentrates in licensing and quantifiers, beyond within-token operators. We resolve both with a small block of sequence quantifiers: a soft existential and a soft proportion, each with a per-unit learned forgetting rate from a sticky init. This recovers the deficit at epoch one (halving the wider epoch-two gap), modestly leads on LAMBADA, and makes the FFN legible: the structure now holds and migrates into depth; the decay un-learns its stickiness (median half-life ~1.5 tokens; zero latch units); and at the semantic layers the units read, without dictionary learning, as grammatical licensing detectors: each fires on a licensor (a comparative, a passive participle, a negative-polarity item) and carries its memory forward to predict the licensed word (than, by, nor). This legibility is localized and free only up to a partition (a fully Boolean FFN diverges in training), but the result is a parameter-neutral, language-model-quality transformer with a readable, interpretable-by-construction grammatical mechanism -- an account not just of what a feed-forward layer represents but how it licenses.

</details>


### [50] [Theory of Mind and Persuasion Beyond Conversation: Assessing the Capacity of LLMs to Induce Belief States via Planning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31916)
*Ben Slater,Matteo G. Mecattaf,Lucy G. Cheke,John Burden,Winnie Street*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出非对话式规划心理理论（NCP-ToM）评估框架NCP-ExploreToM，测试LLM通过行动而非对话诱导他人信念状态的能力。在600个任务实例中评估了6个前沿模型和人类参与者，GPT-5在约80%的任务中成功，是唯一超越人类表现的模型，但鲁棒性仍低于人类。所有模型在诱导真实信念状态任务上表现优于虚假信念状态任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ToM基准通常依赖被动问答格式，但LLM越来越多地被部署为自主智能体形态，需要新的评估范式来测试智能体通过行动而非对话诱导他人信念状态的能力（NCP-ToM），这对于用户助手交互和教学场景至关重要，但也可能带来操纵或 misinformation 风险。

Method: 提出NCP-ExploreToM框架，颠覆传统任务结构：为模型提供一组信念状态目标，要求它们通过移动物体或将角色引导至房间来实现目标。在600个任务实例中评估了6个前沿模型（GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Claude 4系列）和人类参与者。

Result: GPT-5在智能体设定下约80%任务成功，是唯一超越人类表现（在本文任务上）的模型，但鲁棒性仍低于人类。所有模型在诱导真实信念状态任务上表现优于诱导虚假信念状态任务。

Conclusion: LLM展现出新兴的非对话式社会推理能力，但仍有局限。研究发现对对齐工作有积极意义（真实信念优于虚假信念），强调了智能体评估对于理解自主社会智能体的安全性和对齐的必要性。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) benchmarks for Large Language Models (LLMs) typically rely on passive question-answering formats, but the deployment of LLMs in increasingly agentic and autonomous forms demands new evaluations. In this paper we evaluate an agent's ability to induce specific belief states in other agents by taking actions rather than using conversational persuasion, a capability we call Non-Conversational Planning ToM (NCP-ToM). NCP-ToM is likely to be essential for many agent use-cases, including within user-assistant interactions and pedagogical contexts, but may also present manipulation or misinformation risks. Using a novel framework, NCP-ExploreToM, we subvert the conventional task structure by providing models with a set of belief state goals and requiring them to move objects or direct characters into rooms to achieve their goals. We evaluated six frontier models, including GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro and the Claude 4 series, and a cohort of human participants, across 600 task instances. GPT-5 was successful on approximately 80% of tasks in the agentic setting, and was the only model to outperform human participants on our task, but was still less robust than humans across contexts. We additionally found that all models, like humans, performed better on tasks inducing true belief states than false belief states, which is a positive signal for alignment efforts. These findings highlight emerging social-reasoning capabilities in LLMs for non-conversational task completion and underscore the necessity of agentic evaluations for understanding the safety and alignment of autonomous social agents.

</details>


### [51] [LuxEmo: Expressive Text-to-Speech Corpus for Luxembourgish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31947)
*Nina Hosseini-Kivanani,Sandipana Dowerah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LuxEmo，一个21小时的卢森堡语情感语音数据集，包含4种情感类别，并基于半自动筛选流程构建；同时评估了五种情感TTS系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音数据集主要面向广泛使用的语言，低资源语言（如卢森堡语）在语音技术研究中长期被忽视，缺乏情感语音资源。

Method: 采用半自动筛选流程：语音活动检测、去噪、语种识别、LuxASR分割、自动情感预测、词汇线索及人工审核；数据集来源于RTL青年广播节目。在TTS方面，测试了五种系统：基于德语跨语言迁移、多语言卢森堡语支持、卢森堡语适配、非参数韵律迁移。

Result: 构建了21小时、4情感类别的卢森堡语对话情感语料库LuxEmo；在多种TTS系统上进行了客观指标和人工评估。

Conclusion: LuxEmo填补了卢森堡语情感语音资源的空白，提出的半自动流程可用于其他低资源语言的语料库构建，为低资源语言情感TTS研究提供了基准。

Abstract: State-of-the-art speech datasets predominantly focus on widely spoken languages, often overlooking low-resource languages such as Luxembourgish, which remain underrepresented in speech technology research. In this work, we introduce LuxEmo, a 21-hour conversational expressive speech corpus for Luxembourgish with 4 emotion categories. LuxEmo is derived from Radio Télévision Luxembourg (RTL) youth broadcasts, using automated detection followed by human validation. We propose a semi-automatic curation workflow combining voice activity detection, denoising, language identification, LuxASR-based segmentation, automatic emotion prediction, lexical cues, and targeted human review. Additionally, we benchmark five expressive TTS systems covering German-based cross-lingual transfer, multilingual Luxembourgish support, Luxembourgish adaptation, and non-parametric prosody transfer. Performance is evaluated using both objective metrics and human evaluation.

</details>


### [52] [DigitalCoach: Communication and Grounding Gaps in Human and Agentic Computer Use Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31980)
*Meng Chen,Anya Ji,Tsung-Han Wu,Tobias Maringgele,David M. Chan,Alane Suhr,Amy Pavel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DigitalCoach是一个包含72场人类专家-新手计算机使用辅导会话的多模态数据集（22,752轮对话，28.1小时屏幕和输入事件记录，覆盖5款软件应用），用于评估AI模型能否教会人类使用软件。评估发现：模型倾向于提供直接指令，但缺乏解释、错误诊断和知识检查；即使固定辅导方法，模型生成的语句虽接近人类参考但视觉基础薄弱；交互评估显示模型辅导导致学习者被动跟随指令而缺乏深度参与。DigitalCoach为协作式和主动式的计算机使用辅导代理奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理越来越擅长自动化软件任务，但其能否教会人类自己使用软件这一问题尚未得到充分研究。作者希望通过构建数据集和评估框架来系统性地研究这一问题。

Method: 构建DigitalCoach多模态数据集（72场人类专家-新手辅导会话），通过自动评估（对比模型与人类在辅导策略上的差异）和交互评估（让人类学习者与模型辅导代理互动）两种方式，评估当前最先进模型在计算机使用教学方面的能力。

Result: 自动评估显示模型更多提供直接指令，但更少提供解释、错误诊断和知识检查问题；固定辅导方法后模型语句接近人类但视觉基础差；交互评估表明模型辅导导致学习者被动跟随指令，缺乏深度参与和视觉基础。

Conclusion: DigitalCoach为协作式和主动式计算机使用辅导代理的研究奠定了基础，揭示了当前模型在计算机教学方面的不足——尤其是缺乏解释性、视觉基础性以及促进学习者深度参与的能力。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly capable of automating software tasks, but can they teach humans how to use software themselves? We introduce DigitalCoach, a multimodal dataset of 72 human expert-novice computer use coaching sessions consisting of 22,752 dialogue turns grounded in 28.1 hours of screen and input event recordings across five software applications. We use DigitalCoach to evaluate whether state-of-the-art models can teach humans how to use computers. Automated evaluation shows that models differ from humans in how they coach: models provide more direct instructions, but fewer explanations, error diagnoses, and knowledge-check questions. When we fix the coaching method, models produce utterances similar to human references yet poorly grounded in visual context. Interactive evaluation confirms that model coaches cause learners to passively follow instructions without deeper engagement and fall short in visual grounding. DigitalCoach lays a foundation for collaborative and proactive computer use coaching agents.

</details>


### [53] [Scalable Behaviour Cloning on Browser Using via Skill Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32014)
*Kaisen Yang,Zheng Jiang,Yuzhao Peng,Houde Qian,Boshi Zhang,Youjie Zheng,Shijin Hong,Qingle Liu,Ruoyu Han,Bohan Lyu,Bingxiang He,Eren Cai,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过技能蒸馏（Skill Distillation）将用户浏览器交互轨迹转化为自然语言技能，并构建技能图谱（Skill Graph）来增强浏览器智能体的决策能力，从而实现可扩展的行为克隆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网用户通过浏览器执行大量熟练工作（如软件开发、文档编辑、搜索填表等），这些人类浏览行为是高度可扩展但未被充分利用的可复用浏览器技能来源。现有浏览器智能体的瓶颈在于不完全信息下的决策制定，而非底层操作，而智能体所缺乏的先验知识已隐式存在于人类交互轨迹中。

Method: 采用可扩展的行为克隆方法——技能蒸馏（Skill Distillation），将用户交互轨迹转化为紧凑的自然语言技能，智能体可直接读取、检索、复用和组合这些技能。进一步将蒸馏出的技能组织成技能图谱（Skill Graph），使技能增长通过整合（consolidation）而非无界累积来实现。

Result: 研究表明浏览器智能体的可扩展性可能更多地来源于互联网用户已表达的集体技能，而非人工设计的任务。

Conclusion: 通过从人类交互轨迹中蒸馏技能并构建技能图谱，可以显著提升浏览器智能体在不完全信息下的决策能力，为浏览器智能体的可扩展发展提供了新路径。

Abstract: Internet users collectively perform an enormous range of skilled work through web browsers, from software development and document editing to search, forms, and enterprise workflows, making human browsing a highly scalable but under-exploited source of reusable browser skills. We argue that the bottleneck for browser agents is decision-making under incomplete information rather than low-level operation, and that the priors agents lack are already implicit in human interaction traces. We therefore study scalable behavior cloning for browser agents via skill distillation, converting user interaction trajectories into compact natural-language skills that agents can read, retrieve, reuse, and compose directly. We further organize the distilled skills into a skill graph so that growth proceeds through consolidation rather than unbounded accumulation. This suggests that the scalability of browser agents may come less from manually designed tasks and more from the collective skills already expressed by internet users. Our project is available at: https://lab.einsia.ai/browserbc/.

</details>


### [54] [Generative Skill Composition for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32025)
*Xinyu Zhao,Zhen Tan,Vaishnav Tadiparthi,Nakul Agarwal,Kwonjoon Lee,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillComposer方法，将技能组合形式化为结构化技能组合问题，通过约束自回归解码器联合预测技能子集、数量和顺序，在GPT-5.2-Codex和Gemini-3-Pro-Preview上分别提升通过率+23.1和+18.2个百分点，优于top-3检索并匹配黄金技能检索上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在技能库选择上存在瓶颈：要么将整个技能库暴露给推理过程，要么通过embedding或LLM重排序进行检索，但都忽略了技能组合的结构化本质——技能选择、数量和顺序是三个不可解耦的联合决策维度。

Method: SkillComposer将结构化技能组合实例化为任务条件化的技能序列预测，使用约束自回归解码器对技能标识符进行解码，子集、数量和执行顺序在一次解码过程中联合生成，自然捕获连续技能间的依赖关系。训练数据来自真实人工策展的技能库。

Result: 在GPT-5.2-Codex和Gemini-3-Pro-Preview上，SkillComposer相比无技能基线分别提升+23.1和+18.2个百分点，超越top-3检索方法，以更低的提示词token成本匹配黄金技能检索上界。

Conclusion: 结构化技能组合将技能选择、数量和顺序作为联合决策问题，通过序列预测范式有效提升了多智能体系统的任务完成率，同时降低了推理成本。

Abstract: Recent LLM agents benefit from skills for solving complex tasks. Skills encapsulate modular packages of procedural knowledge and instructions for performing specialized tasks, such as setting up a sandboxed environment, running a test suite, or refactoring a function across multiple files. As skill libraries grow and become reusable across tasks and domains, selecting an appropriate skill composition has emerged as a central bottleneck. Existing approaches fall into two categories. One exposes the agent's reasoning to the entire skill collection; the other performs skill retrieval via embeddings or LLM-based rerankers. Both provide useful insights; however, they miss the structural nature of skill composition, which is a joint decision over which skills, how many, and in what order -- three dimensions that cannot be decoupled. We formalize this as structured skill composition: given a task and a skill library, predict an executable skill plan that jointly specifies the activated subset, count, and execution order. We propose SkillComposer, which instantiates structured skill composition as task-conditioned skill sequence prediction. SkillComposer uses a constrained autoregressive decoder over skill identifiers, so subset, count, and order emerge jointly from a single decoding pass, and dependencies between successive skills are captured naturally. We build a training set of task-composition pairs from a real, human-curated skill library. We then evaluate SkillComposer along two axes: composition quality on a held-out test set, and downstream task success on SkillsBench across two production-grade coding agents. On GPT-5.2-Codex, Gemini-3-Pro-Preview, SkillComposer raises the pass rate by +23.1, +18.2pp over the no-skill baseline, surpassing top-3 retrieval and matching the gold-skill retrieval upper bound at lower prompt-token cost.

</details>


### [55] [When LLMs Read Tables Carelessly: Measuring and Reducing Data Referencing Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32029)
*Yuqing Yang,Qi Zhu,Zhen Han,Boran Han,Zhengyuan Shen,Shuai Wang,Vassilis N. Ioannidis,Huzefa Rangwala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了大语言模型在表格任务中的数据引用错误（DREs），发现所有模型（1.7B-20B）均存在此类问题；提出将数据引用作为评判标准，通过评判过滤和拒绝采样将准确率提升12.0%；并训练了一个4B参数的轻量评判模型，在检测分布内和分布外DREs上F1达78.2%，有效辅助大模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在表格任务中虽然理解表格结构，但仍会出现数据引用错误（DREs），即错误引用或遗漏表格数值。这类错误会直接影响中间推理步骤的正确性和可靠性，但此前研究仅提供了有限的、小规模的分析，缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 1) 首次跨模型（1.7B至20B参数）、跨任务系统评估DREs；2) 提出将数据引用作为评判标准（data referencing as a critic），通过评判过滤和拒绝采样提升答案准确率；3) 训练一个4B参数的轻量评判模型用于检测DREs。

Result: 1) DREs在所有测试模型中普遍存在；2) 通过评判过滤和拒绝采样，答案准确率最高提升12.0%；3) 4B评判模型在检测分布内和分布外DREs上平均F1得分78.2%，并能有效辅助更大模型的推理。

Conclusion: 数据引用错误是影响LLM表格推理可靠性的关键问题，而专门训练的轻量评判模型可以有效检测并缓解该问题，提升大模型在表格任务中的表现。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) perform well on table tasks, they still make data referencing errors (DREs), i.e., incorrectly citing or omitting table values, despite understanding the table structure. Beyond final-answer accuracy, DREs directly compromise the correctness and reliability of intermediate reasoning steps. Yet prior studies have only offered limited, small-scale analyses. In this work, we present the first systematic evaluation of tabular data referencing errors across different models and tasks. Our results show that DREs occur across all tested models (1.7B to 20B parameters). Furthermore, we demonstrate that incorporating data referencing as a critic significantly improves answer accuracy up to 12.0%, through critic-based filtering and rejection sampling. Finally, we trained a lightweight 4B-parameter critic model that achieves an average F1 score of 78.2% in detecting both in-distribution and out-of-distribution DREs, and effectively assists inference for larger models.

</details>


### [56] [Reinforcement Learning with Metacognitive Feedback Elicits Faithful Uncertainty Expression in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32032)
*Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Avi Caciularu,Gal Yona,Idan Szpektor,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种元认知增强机制——基于元认知反馈的强化学习（RLMF）和元认知数据选择，用于提升大语言模型的忠实校准能力，即对齐模型表达的不确定性与内在不确定性。实验表明RLMF在多种任务上达到SOTA，比标准RL提升高达63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在元认知能力上存在系统性缺陷：高置信度幻觉、无法识别知识边界、错误表达内在不确定性，这损害了可信赖性和可靠性。现有方法难以使模型准确判断自身表现并据此改进行为。

Method: 1) RLMF：在偏好优化阶段，基于模型对自身表现的自我判断质量来精炼完成排序的强化学习范式；2) 元认知数据选择：利用模型自我判断识别高价值训练样本，优于朴素主动学习。采用两阶段解耦方法：先校准置信度，再通过目标输出编辑映射到自然语言不确定性表达。

Result: RLMF在多种任务上实现可泛化的SOTA忠实校准，同时保持准确性；比标准RL提升高达63%；增强了模型评估和表达自身能力边界的能力。

Conclusion: RLMF作为增强LLM元认知的有前景范式，可将元认知性能作为有效的RL信号，克服先前内在反馈方法的局限性，提升模型能力与对齐性。

Abstract: Metacognition is a critical component of intelligence that describes the ability to monitor and regulate one's own cognitive processes. Yet LLMs exhibit systemic deficiencies in key metacognitive faculties: they hallucinate with high confidence, fail to recognize knowledge boundaries, and misrepresent their internal uncertainty--undermining trustworthiness and reliability. Since monitoring task performance and adapting behavior accordingly are central to metacognition, we posit that models capable of accurately judging their own performance are better positioned to improve it. We operationalize this idea via two novel mechanisms: reinforcement learning with metacognitive feedback (RLMF), a paradigm to refine completion rankings during preference optimization based on the quality of a model's self-judgments of performance, and metacognitive data selection, which uses similar self-judgments to identify high-value training examples, outperforming naive active learning. We apply these innovations to the problem of faithful calibration (FC), a task that is itself fundamentally metacognitive: the goal is to align expressed with intrinsic uncertainty, difficult even for frontier LLMs. We adopt a two-stage, decoupled approach, first using these methods to calibrate the faithfulness of models' self-reported confidence scores, then mapping to natural, context-adaptable linguistic uncertainty via targeted output editing. Extensive experiments show RLMF achieves generalizable, state-of-the-art FC on diverse tasks while preserving accuracy. Further, RLMF surpasses standard RL by up to 63% while enhancing models' ability to assess and express their own capability limits. This positions RLMF as a promising paradigm to enhance LLM metacognition toward improved abilities and alignment, and suggests metacognitive performance as an effective RL signal to overcome limits of prior intrinsic feedback methods.

</details>


### [57] [Introspective Coupling: Self-Explanation Training Tracks Behavioral Change Despite Fixed Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32038)
*Zifan Carl Guo,Laura Ruis,Jacob Andreas,Belinda Z. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，训练语言模型生成解释（解释输入特征如何影响行为）时，即使使用固定且来自早期检查点或其他模型家族的解释数据，模型仍能产生与其当前行为高度“内省”一致的忠实解释。这种内省耦合在解释与当前行为保持相关性时出现，并能追踪行为变化，为可扩展的后训练内省信号提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究训练语言模型生成解释（基于反事实输入的行为监督）时，产生的解释究竟是忠实的内省（faithful introspection）还是肤浅的模仿（superficial imitation），即模型是否真正内省其行为原因。

Method: 训练LM生成解释，监督信号为模型在修改输入后的反事实行为；使用来自模型自身早期检查点或其他行为相似但不同家族模型的固定反事实解释作为训练数据，评估解释与当前行为的忠实度；在多个任务（如谄媚、拒绝）上验证，并加入后训练目标与标签噪声测试。

Result: 使用固定解释训练的LM，其生成的解释对自身当前行为的忠实度高于对训练目标行为的忠实度；内省耦合在训练过程中解释与当前行为保持足够相关性时出现；当与其他后训练目标同时进行时，解释能追踪行为变化而无需更新监督；该现象在多个任务上成立且对标签噪声鲁棒。

Conclusion: 即使使用固定的反事实解释数据集，也能为后训练提供可扩展且可泛化的内省信号，使LM产生更忠实于自身行为的解释。

Abstract: When does training language models (LMs) to generate explanations of their predictions yield faithful introspection, rather than superficial imitation? We study LMs trained to explain which features of their inputs influenced their behavior, using models' counterfactual behavior on modified inputs as supervision. Surprisingly, we find that LMs trained on fixed counterfactual explanations derived from earlier checkpoints of themselves, or even from behaviorally similar models in different families, frequently produce explanations more faithful to their own current behaviors than to those of their training targets. This "introspective" coupling between LM explanations and behaviors occurs when training explanations remain sufficiently correlated with current behaviors over the course of training, even as behaviors themselves shift. We also show that introspective coupling tracks behavior shifts: when explanation training is provided concurrently with other post-training objectives, explanations track those shifts without requiring updated supervision. This phenomenon appears in multiple tasks, including sycophancy and refusal, and is robust to label noise. Overall, our results show that even fixed datasets of counterfactual explanations can provide scalable and generalizable post-training signal for introspection.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

### [58] [SpikON: A Dual-Parallel and Efficient Accelerator for Online Spiking Neural Networks Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30926)
*Peilin Chen,Xiaoxuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SpikON是首个面向高效可扩展的端到端在线监督SNN学习的算法-硬件协同设计框架，通过可学习阈值、缩放权重集中化、级联计算复用等技术，在保持精度的同时显著降低训练延迟和能耗，比边缘GPU和TPU实现7.2倍至26.8倍的训练吞吐量/能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线无监督SNN学习存在训练精度低、可扩展性差的问题；在线监督学习虽在大规模数据集上表现良好，但非硬件友好的操作阻碍了高效的边缘部署。

Method: 提出可学习阈值（Learnable Threshold through Time）和缩放权重集中化（Scaled Weight Centralization through Time）技术；引入训练数据流和级联计算复用方案，实现前向-反向并行计算和时间步间复用；设计双并行引擎和定制SIMD的SNN加速器。

Result: 算法层面：训练延迟和能耗分别降低32.2%和35.0%，精度无损。协同设计层面：相比Apple M4 GPU和TPU类加速器，训练吞吐量提升7.2倍和11.5倍，能效提升26.8倍和15.8倍。

Conclusion: SpikON通过算法-硬件协同设计，有效解决了在线监督SNN学习的高效性和可扩展性问题，为边缘端SNN部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for energy-efficient brain-inspired computing. However, existing online unsupervised SNN learning suffers from low training accuracy and poor scalability. Although current online supervised learning algorithms perform well on large-scale datasets and networks, the non-hardware-friendly operations hinder efficient edge deployment. In this work, we propose SpikON, the first algorithm-hardware co-design framework for efficient and scalable end-to-end online supervised SNN learning. We first propose the learnable threshold through time and scaled weight centralization through time techniques to address the inefficiency of traditional algorithms. Moreover, to reduce latency and energy consumption, we introduce the novel training dataflow and cascade computation reuse scheme for SNNs that allows concurrent forward-backward computation and temporal reuse across timesteps. We further design the dedicated SNN accelerator with a dual-parallel engine and customized SIMD-based SNN core for efficient end-to-end online learning. Experiments show that the SpikON algorithm achieves 32.2% and 35.0% reductions in training latency and energy consumption over the baseline, without sacrificing accuracy. Moreover, the SpikON co-design achieves 7.2x (11.5x) and 26.8x (15.8x) training throughput (energy efficiency) compared with the edge Apple M4 GPU and TPU-like accelerator, respectively. The code is available at https://github.com/peilin-chen/SpikON.

</details>


### [59] [Dynamic Ultrasound Beamforming Using Left-to-Right Arithmetic Adders on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31490)
*Muhammad Usman,Shujaat Khan,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于从左到右/最高有效位优先(MSDF)算术的加法树架构，替代传统全精度并行加法树用于超声延迟叠加波束形成。在Xilinx Zynq XC7Z010 FPGA上实现表明，所提设计相比最小传统树节省2.5倍LUT，满足时序约束，比最高效传统基线功耗低23%，且支持动态精度缩放，等面积复制可实现15个并行实例，达到67 FPS，吞吐量比最佳传统设计高80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统并行加法树在延迟叠加超声波束形成中存在全精度组合逻辑缩减导致的关键路径宽、LUT消耗高、在小型FPGA上时序违例等问题，需要一种更高效的替代方案。

Method: 采用从左到右(LR)或最高有效位优先(MSDF)算术构建加法树架构，替代传统的全精度并行加法树。在Xilinx Zynq XC7Z010 FPGA上实现并对比评估，使用64通道超声数据集进行DAS波束形成测试。

Result: 所提设计LUT消耗比最小传统树少2.5倍；满足时序约束；动态功耗比最高效传统基线低23%；通过停止计算时钟实现运行时动态精度缩放，控制开销可忽略；等面积复制下实现15个并行实例，达到67 FPS，吞吐量比最佳传统设计高80%。

Conclusion: MSDF加法树在资源效率、功耗、时序和吞吐量方面均优于传统并行加法树，其天然支持动态精度缩放带来质量-能量可伸缩性，这是传统固定周期加法树无法实现的。

Abstract: Adder trees are the computational backbone of delay-and-sum (DAS) ultrasound beamforming, where their implementation directly determines the energy, throughput, and area of a real-time imaging pipeline. Conventional parallel adder trees perform full-precision combinational reduction on every sample, leading to wide critical paths, high LUT consumption, and timing failures on small FPGA devices. This paper presents an alternative adder tree architecture based on \emph{left-to-right (LR)} or \emph{most significant digit first (MSDF) arithmetic}. We implement the proposed and conventional adder trees on a Xilinx Zynq XC7Z010 FPGA and evaluate them for DAS beamforming of a 64-channel ultrasound dataset. The proposed design uses 2.5$\times$ fewer LUTs than the smallest conventional tree, successfully meets the timing constraint, and consumes 23\% less dynamic power than the most efficient conventional baseline. A key advantage of the proposed MSDF adder tree is that it can generate high-quality beamformed images without waiting for full-precision completion. This naturally enables dynamic precision at runtime with negligible control overhead, since precision selection is achieved simply by stopping the computation clock after the desired number of cycles. Such quality--energy scalability is fundamentally unavailable in conventional fixed-cycle adder trees. Iso-area replication enables up to 15 parallel instances on the XC7Z010, achieving 67 FPS, which is 80\% higher throughput than the best conventional design.

</details>


### [60] [MINT: Dynamic-Precision CNN Inference with MSDF Digit-Serial Arithmetic on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31514)
*Muhammad Usman,Malik Zohaib Nisar,Florian Aschauer,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: MINT是一种基于从左到右（LR）算术的动态精度CNN推理加速器，通过最高有效位优先计算实现早期终止，在VGG-16和ResNet-18上平均仅用5.64和6.04位，相比INT8基线吞吐量提升32.6%、能效提升82.10%，且精度损失控制在2%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CNN推理加速器在精度与效率之间难以取得最佳平衡，固定精度计算（如INT8）存在冗余计算；需要一种能根据每层实际需求动态调整精度的方案，以在不显著损失精度的情况下最大化计算效率和能效。

Method: 提出MINT加速器，核心是基于最高有效位优先（MSDF）的串行-并行内积单元，采用冗余有符号数表示（SD表示）逐卷积窗口计算；通过预算约束的贪心搜索算法，对VGG-16和ResNet-18各卷积层从INT2到INT7进行精度配置搜索，在总精度损失不超过INT8基线2%的约束下选择每层最低精度。

Result: 在Xilinx Zynq-7020上综合实现，工作频率200MHz；VGG-16平均5.64位，ResNet-18平均6.04位；VGG-16达到19.86 GOPS和29.51 GOPS/W，ResNet-18达到18.86 GOPS和26.40 GOPS/W；相比INT8基线，吞吐量分别提升32.6%和26.0%，能效分别提升82.10%和62.90%，精度仅下降1.81%和1.96%。在同平台对比中，MINT在VGG-16和ResNet-18设计上均取得最高能效。

Conclusion: MINT验证了基于LR算术的动态精度策略在CNN加速中的有效性，能显著提升吞吐量和能效而几乎不损失精度；该方法为资源受限平台上的高效CNN推理提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: We present MINT, a dynamic-precision CNN inference accelerator based on left-to-right (LR) arithmetic. LR arithmetic computes in most-significant-digit-first manner and exposes useful partial results early so that the computation can be terminated once the desired precision is achieved. At the core, there is a MSDF serial-parallel inner-product unit, which uses redundant signed-digit representation to compute each convolution window. A budget-constrained greedy search profiles all convolution layers from INT2 to INT7 and selects the lowest precision per layer while constraining total accuracy loss to within 2\% of the INT8 baseline for VGG-16 and ResNet-18 networks. The design is synthesized on a Xilinx Zynq-7020 at \SI{200}{\mega\hertz}, and uses 5.64 average bits for VGG-16 and 6.04 for ResNet-18, while achieving 19.86 GOPS and 29.51 GOPS/W on VGG-16, and 18.86 GOPS and 26.40 GOPS/W on ResNet-18. This corresponds to 32.6\% and 26.0\% higher throughput and 82.10\% and 62.90\% higher energy efficiency than INT8 with only 1.81\% and 1.96\% drops relative to the INT8 baseline. Compared with representative prior FPGA CNN accelerators considered in this study, MINT delivers the highest energy efficiency among the listed VGG-16 and ResNet-18 designs on Zynq-7020 platform.

</details>


### [61] [PEERS: A Parallel and Exact Effective Resistance Solver via Implicit Inversion and Augmented Symbolic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31535)
*Baiyu Chen,Lin Gan,Guangwen Yang,Wenjian Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出PEERS，一种基于Cholesky因子隐式逆计算模型的并行精确有效电阻求解器，通过状态继承的增广深度优先搜索和动态查询更新机制，在保持O(nnz(L))空间复杂度的同时实现最优并行跨度O(n^α)，在工业基准测试中平均加速83.3倍，首次实现百万节点级电路的全边精确电阻分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精确有效电阻计算方法面临"精度-内存困境"：近似方法无法满足工业签核的严格精度要求，精确方法要么存在冗余查询开销，要么引发O(n²)内存爆炸，亟需一种兼顾高精度、低内存和高并行效率的求解方案。

Method: 基于Cholesky因子的隐式逆计算模型，结合状态继承的增广深度优先搜索（DFS）与动态查询更新机制，在单次并行扫描中完成所有边电阻查询，并利用图分离器定理实现最优并行跨度。

Result: 在工业基准测试中平均加速比达83.3倍，处理百万节点工业图仅需18.8秒，可扩展至1700万节点并在1小时内完成，严格保持O(nnz(L))空间复杂度。

Conclusion: PEERS首次为百万门级设计的全边精确电阻分析提供了计算上可行的路径，突破了EDA签核中精度与内存的瓶颈。

Abstract: High-precision effective resistance computation is a cornerstone of Electronic Design Automation (EDA) sign-off, yet it remains a fundamental bottleneck in large-scale power grid analysis, spectral sparsification, and circuit reliability. Existing approaches face a prohibitive "precision-memory impasse": approximate methods lack the stringent accuracy required for high-stakes industrial sign-off, while exact methods either suffer from redundant query overheads or trigger $O(n^2)$ memory explosions. To resolve this, we propose PEERS, a Parallel and Exact Effective Resistance Solver powered by an implicit inverse computing model of the Cholesky factor. By integrating a state-inherited augmented depth-first search (DFS) with a dynamic query update mechanism, PEERS eliminates numerical redundancy and evaluates all-edge resistance queries in a single parallel sweep. We provide a rigorous Work-Span analysis, proving that for graphs satisfying an $O(n^α)$ separator theorem, PEERS achieves a theoretically optimal parallel span of $O(n^α)$ while strictly maintaining $O(nnz(L))$ space complexity. Numerical evaluations on industrial benchmarks demonstrate that PEERS achieves an average speedup of 83.3x over state-of-the-art parallel solvers under identical memory constraints. Notably, PEERS processes a 1-million-node industrial graph in just 18.8 seconds and scales to 17 million nodes in under an hour, providing the first computationally feasible path for exact all-edge resistance analysis in multi-million-gate designs.

</details>


### [62] [In-situ Indexing via Memristive Content-Addressable Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31554)
*Bing Wu,Xueliang Wei,Shiyi Song,Yibo Liu,Jinpeng Liu,Wei Tong,Hao Tong,Yuchong Hu,Dan Feng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: PATH提出了一种基于处理中内存(PIM)的原位索引架构，通过将基本索引操作重构为高并行的原位内容可寻址内存操作，解决了内存墙问题。以哈希索引为例，PATH采用超大逻辑桶和内存移动技术，几乎消除了哈希冲突解决成本并显著降低了重哈希开销，相比现有方案实现了4.7-7.8倍吞吐量提升、>14.5倍尾延迟降低和>61.4%内存访问减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管PIM已被证明是克服"内存墙"的有效范式，但数据索引同样受到内存墙的严重制约，而索引如何有效利用PIM的独特能力仍不清楚。

Method: 将Insert、Search、Update、Delete等基本索引操作重构为直接在内存阵列内执行的高并行原位内容可寻址内存(CAM)操作；以哈希索引为例，采用超大逻辑桶和内存移动技术。

Result: 相比现有方案，PATH实现了4.7-7.8倍吞吐量提升、>14.5倍尾延迟降低、>61.4%内存访问减少。

Conclusion: PATH几乎消除了哈希冲突解决成本并显著降低了重哈希开销，为下一代数据密集型计算奠定了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Processing-in-Memory (PIM) is a proven paradigm for overcoming the ``memory wall". However, while data indexing is severely bottlenecked by this same wall, it remains unclear how indexing can effectively benefit from PIM's unique capabilities. We present PATH, an in-situ indexing architecture that bridges this gap by leveraging the massive parallelism and inherent data-movement of PIMs. Specifically, we first reformulate the fundamental indexing operations, namely Insert, Search, Update, and Delete, into highly parallel in-situ content-addressable memory operations executed directly within memory arrays. Taking hash indexes as a typical case, we elaborate how PATH breaks the inherent trade-off among memory accesses, load factor, and process latency in conventional hashing schemes. By adopting ultra-large logical buckets and in-memory moving, PATH virtually eliminates the cost of hash collision resolution and significantly reduces resizing overhead. Compared with state-of-the-art schemes, PATH achieves $4.7-7.8\times$ higher throughput, $>14.5\times$ lower tail latency, and $>61.4\%$ fewer memory accesses under insertions, laying a scalable foundation for next-generation data-centric computing.

</details>


### [63] [FlexViT: A Flexible FPGA-based Accelerator for Edge Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31938)
*Hubert Dymarkowski,Xingjian Fu,Rappy Saha,Jude Haris,José Cano*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FlexViT是一种面向资源受限边缘设备的可重构FPGA加速器，通过硬件-软件协同设计、双模式数据流和深度优先分块策略，有效加速混合架构ViT模型推理，在PYNQ-Z2 FPGA上实现最高2.74倍层加速和1.40倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘平台上部署Vision Transformer模型面临高计算需求和混合架构（全连接层与卷积层并存）导致的张量形状异构问题，现有方案缺乏灵活高效的FPGA加速支持。

Method: 基于SECDA-TFLite框架，采用硬件-软件协同设计：1）将全连接和卷积层统一映射到高吞吐INT8 GEMM引擎，通过运行时im2col变换实现；2）提出双模式数据流，通过运行时重构计算阵列在输入重用和权重重用间动态切换；3）提出深度优先分块策略，单次遍历完成累加，消除片外部分和传输。

Result: 在PYNQ-Z2 FPGA上，对多种ViT模型评估，加速器执行层获得最高2.74倍加速，端到端推理获得最高1.40倍加速（对比纯CPU执行）。

Conclusion: FlexViT通过可重构设计和高效数据流调度，有效解决了边缘设备上混合ViT模型的FPGA加速挑战，实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Deploying Vision Transformer (ViT) models on edge platforms remains challenging due to their high computational demands and the architectural heterogeneity of modern hybrid ViT models, which incorporate both fully connected and convolutional layers. This heterogeneity leads to significant variation in tensor shapes, requiring flexible and efficient FPGA-based acceleration. In this paper, we present FlexViT, a reconfigurable FPGA accelerator for efficient ViT inference on resource-constrained edge devices. Built on the SECDA-TFLite framework, FlexViT employs a hardware-software co-design approach that maps both fully connected and convolutional layers onto a unified high-throughput INT8 GEMM engine using a runtime im2col transformation. To efficiently support diverse layer configurations, we propose a dual-mode dataflow that dynamically switches between input and weight reuse by reconfiguring the compute array at runtime. We further introduce a depth-first tiling strategy that completes accumulation in a single pass, eliminating off-chip partial-sum transfers and reducing memory bandwidth requirements. We implement FlexViT on a PYNQ-Z2 FPGA and evaluate it across a representative set of ViT models. FlexViT achieves up to 2.74x speedup on accelerator-executed layers, translating into up to 1.40x end-to-end speedup compared to CPU-only execution. The code is available at: https://github.com/gicLAB/FlexViT

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [64] [Protecting Futures against Silent Data Corruption -- Efficient Task Replication for Dynamic Data Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30771)
*Rüdiger Nather,Claudia Fohry,Mia Reitz*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在异步多任务运行时系统中通过紧密耦合原始任务与副本任务、交叉验证所有对外效应来应对静默数据损坏的方法，仅需选择性重计算受影响任务，实现了低于两倍的故障无运行时间开销，且故障修正开销约为总运行时间的0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着计算问题规模增长，静默数据损坏（SDC）概率增加；现有SDC保护方案多假设静态任务与依赖关系，难以应对动态任务生成、通信及集群负载均衡等复杂场景。

Method: 提出一种将原始计算与副本计算紧密耦合的方法，通过运行时系统交互时交叉验证所有对外效应（如promise/future通信、条件触碰等），仅选择性重计算受影响的任务，而非整个计算图。

Result: 在ItoyoriFBC运行时系统上的实验表明：完全复制下故障无运行时间增加不到两倍（主要得益于任务数量增加带来的负载均衡改善），故障修正开销约为总运行时间的0.5%。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效应对动态AMT运行时系统中的SDC问题，通过交叉验证和选择性重计算实现了可接受的性能开销。

Abstract: As the size of computational problems grows, so does the likelihood of Silent Data Corruptions (SDCs). A common defense is replication, where the computation is repeated and correct results are determined by majority voting. Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) runtimes are generally well suited for this approach, since the inputs and outputs of task replicas can be compared, and the tasks can be recomputed if necessary. Most existing SDC protection schemes assume static tasks and dependencies. Dynamic settings are more challenging, especially in clusters, since the tasks/data must be tracked for the comparisons.
  This paper considers a particularly dynamic setting with task spawning at runtime, task communication through C++11-like promises/futures, conditional touches, and cluster-wide load balancing via work-first work stealing. We propose an approach that closely couples original and replica computations by cross-validating all outgoing effects when interacting with the runtime system. The approach selectively recomputes affected tasks only.
  We implemented the approach in the ItoyoriFBC runtime system and conducted preliminary experiments with Fibonacci and emulated $\mathcal{H}$-matrix LU decomposition benchmarks. Results show a factor of less than two increase of failure-free running times, despite full replication, which is mainly due to improved opportunities for load balancing resulting from the higher number of tasks. The overhead for failure correction was about 0.5% of the overall running time per SDC.

</details>


### [65] [StreamGuard: Low-Overhead Resilience for Real-time HPC Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30848)
*Hai Duc Nguyen,Bogdan Nicolae,Tekin Bicer,Amal Gueroudji,Matthieu Dorier,Kyle Chard,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对实时科学工作流中生产者-消费者流式模式，提出动态异步非阻塞检查点机制和进度感知的负载重分配策略，在故障和性能异常下保持前向进度和负载均衡，开销低于1%，故障影响降低6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时科学工作流运行在复杂、易故障的基础设施上，硬件故障、网络中断及资源争用导致的性能异常会严重违反实时约束，现有方法缺乏针对生产者-消费者流式模式的高效韧性机制。

Method: 提出两种互补技术：(i) 动态、异步、非阻塞检查点机制，在不中断计算的前提下保持进度；(ii) 进度感知的负载重分配策略，检测慢速工作者并主动重新平衡任务。

Result: 故障和性能异常的影响降低最多6倍，无故障执行时开销低于1%。

Conclusion: 所提机制能在高错误率环境下维持前向进度和均衡执行，为实时科学流式工作流提供了轻量级且高效的韧性保障。

Abstract: Real-time scientific workflows operate on continuous data streams and must produce timely, high-quality results despite executing on complex, failure-prone infrastructure. Hardware faults, network disruptions, and performance anomalies caused by resource contention or system heterogeneity can severely degrade performance and violate real-time constraints. We focus on strengthening the resilience of the producer-consumer streaming pattern, a fundamental building block of scientific streaming workflows. We present two complementary techniques: (i) a dynamic, asynchronous, non-blocking checkpointing mechanism that preserves progress without interrupting computation, and (ii) a progress-aware load redistribution strategy that detects slow workers and proactively rebalances tasks. Together, these mechanisms maintain forward progress and balanced execution even in highly error-prone environments. Experimental results show that our approach reduces the impact of failures and performance anomalies by up to 6x, while introducing less than 1% overhead in failure-free execution.

</details>


### [66] [Towards Transparent Checkpointing with AI-driven Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30921)
*Hai Duc Nguyen,Tekin Bicer,Kyle Chard,Ian Foster,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文探索了大型语言模型（Anthropic Claude Opus 4.7）能否端到端自动为MPI科学应用程序添加检查点/重启功能，无需人工干预。在6个科学应用上，LLM平均50分钟生成可用代码，消耗340万token，生成的代码在5个应用中运行时开销可忽略，且能从进程故障中高效恢复，与人工实现的检查点/重启效率相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为MPI科学应用添加可靠的检查点/重启支持需要深厚的专业知识和大量人工投入，是一项耗时的专家级工作。作者希望探索前沿大语言模型能否在无需人工干预的情况下，端到端地自动完成这一任务。

Method: 构建了一个涵盖不同领域和计算模式的MPI应用基准测试套件，使用Anthropic Claude Opus 4.5通过OpenCode CLI驱动迭代式代码生成循环，为每个应用自动生成检查点/重启代码。

Result: 在6个科学应用中，LLM平均50分钟生成可工作的检查点/重启代码，每应用消耗340万token。5个应用上生成的代码在正常无故障运行时开销可忽略，且从注入的进程故障中恢复的效率与人工实现的检查点/重启相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对于相当一部分HPC应用，自动化端到端的LLM驱动的弹性工程在技术上是可行的。

Abstract: Adding reliable checkpoint/restart support to an MPI scientific application is a time-consuming expert effort that requires deep knowledge of both the application and resilience. We ask whether a frontier large language model can perform this work end-to-end without human intervention. We assemble a benchmark suite of MPI applications spanning diverse domains and computation patterns, and drive an iterative code-generation loop for each application using Anthropic's Claude Opus 4.7 invoked through the OpenCode CLI. Across six scientific applications, the LLM generates working checkpoint/restart code in 50 minutes on average while consuming 3.4 M tokens per application. The generated code adds negligible overhead during normal failure-free execution on five of six applications and recovers from injected process failures with efficiency comparable to human-engineered checkpoint/restart implementations. These results suggest that automated end-to-end LLM-driven resilience engineering is technically viable today for a meaningful fraction of HPC applications.

</details>


### [67] [Omni-Flow: A Unified Workflow Orchestration and Distributed KV Cache Sharing Framework for Multimodal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31093)
*Bin Xiao,Jingfu Dong,Changran Wang,Yitian Chen,Xiaoyu Zhao,Yuqi Peng,Jianping Lin,Yuchen Xie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Omni-Flow 是一个面向多模态推理的分布式调度框架，通过三层抽象（控制流、数据流、计算流）统一编排异构计算单元、高效传输中间数据并共享 KV 缓存与模型权重，解决了现有方案在编排灵活性、传输效率和内存冗余方面的核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推理系统将 LLM 和扩散模型独立部署，缺乏系统级抽象，导致编排逻辑分散、传输路径与特定模型紧耦合、集成新模型成本高。具体面临三大挑战：(1) 异构计算单元的灵活编排与复杂依赖/并发控制；(2) 跨进程/节点的海量中间数据高效传输；(3) 跨角色的 KV 缓存和模型权重高效共享以消除 GPU 内存冗余。

Method: 提出三层抽象架构：(1) Control Flow 层：基于 Python DSL 定义工作流，将异构单元编排为统一数据流图，支持静态 DAG 和动态路由，内建服务发现与多种负载均衡策略；(2) Data Flow 层：提供超越 prefill/decode 分离的分布式 KV 缓存抽象，在三层分页存储层级（GPU/CPU/SSD）上统一分配并通过零拷贝低延迟通道实现跨角色直接传输；(3) Compute Flow 层：支持多轮对话中的复杂多模态前缀匹配以复用 KV，通过统一 SGLang 接口接管 KV 缓存和采样逻辑，让扩散模型在统一并行语义下直接复用 LLM 前向路径。

Result: 支持多种异构场景，包括全模态对话（LongCat-Next）和复杂图像生成流水线（HunyuanImage-3），以一致的编程模型完成部署。

Conclusion: Omni-Flow 通过三层抽象实现了多模态推理系统的统一编排、高效通信和内存共享，降低了集成新模型的成本，验证了该框架在多样化异构场景下的适用性和一致性。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) inference evolves from text-only to multimodal paradigms, inference systems face three challenges: (1) flexible orchestration of multimodal workflows, where heterogeneous computing units exhibit complex dependencies and concurrent control; (2) efficient transmission of massive intermediate data across processes and nodes, with tensors flowing at high speed among heterogeneous roles; and (3) efficient sharing of KV caches and model weights across roles to eliminate redundant GPU memory. Existing solutions deploy LLMs and diffusion models independently, lacking a system-level abstraction for multimodal pipelines; this scatters orchestration logic, tightly couples transmission paths to specific models, and incurs high cost to integrate new models. To address these challenges, we present Omni-Flow, a distributed scheduling framework for multimodal inference through a three-layer abstraction. The Control Flow layer defines workflows via a Python DSL, orchestrating heterogeneous units into a unified dataflow graph that supports static DAGs and dynamic routing, with built-in service discovery and diverse load-balancing strategies. The Data Flow layer provides a distributed KV cache abstraction beyond prefill/decode separation, unifying allocation and enabling direct cross-role transmission across a three-tier paged storage hierarchy (GPU/CPU/SSD) over zero-copy, low-latency channels. The Compute Flow layer supports complex multimodal prefix matching for KV reuse across multi-turn dialogues, and takes over KV cache and sampling logic via a unified SGLang interface, letting diffusion models directly reuse the LLM forward path under unified parallel semantics. We demonstrate that Omni-Flow supports diverse heterogeneous scenarios with a consistent programming model, including omni-modal dialogue (LongCat-Next) and complex image generation pipelines (HunyuanImage-3).

</details>


### [68] [AC$^2$P$^2$SL: Adaptive Communication-Computation Pipeline Parallel Split Learning over Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31276)
*Chenyu Liu,Zhaoyang Zhang,Zirui Chen,Zhaohui Yang,Chunhui Feng,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AC$^2$P$^2$SL框架，通过将通信与计算过程流水线化，实现跨微批次的细粒度流水线并行，有效重叠通信与计算，显著减少无线边缘网络中基于分割学习的模型训练时延，并针对系统约束与用户异构性提出了联合优化与自适应重分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分割学习（SL）中计算与通信过程固有的顺序执行导致训练时间过长，限制了其在无线边缘网络中的应用效率。

Method: 提出AC$^2$P$^2$SL框架，将用户设备（UE）与基站（BS）的通信和计算过程统一建模为流水线，实现跨多个微批次的细粒度流水线并行；考虑通信、计算、存储维度系统约束及UE异构性，构建联合优化问题最小化训练时间，并提出分割与预分配算法；针对动态环境设计自适应重分配策略以增强系统韧性。

Result: 大量实验结果表明AC$^2$P$^2$SL在显著减少训练时间的同时有效保障了数据隐私。

Conclusion: AC$^2$P$^2$SL通过流水线并行与优化分配策略，有效克服了传统SL顺序执行导致的时延瓶颈，在动态无线边缘网络中兼具高效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: In wireless edge networks, split learning (SL) enables base station (BS) to utilize the distributed data and computing power across user equipments (UEs) to achieve collaborative model training while protecting local data privacy. However, the inherent sequential execution of computation and communication processes in conventional SL usually leads to long training times. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes an adaptive communication-computation pipeline parallel split learning (AC$^2$P$^2$SL) framework. By conceptualizing the communication and computation processes of UEs and the BS as a unified pipeline, AC$^2$P$^2$SL achieves fine-grained pipeline parallelism across multiple micro-batches. Through this approach, effective overlapping of communication and computation is achieved which results in significant reduction of the overall training latency. Moreover, by considering the system constraints in the communication, computation, and storage dimensions as well as the heterogeneity of UEs, we formulate a joint optimization problem to minimize the training time and propose a corresponding split and pre-allocation algorithm to further enhance the pipeline efficiency. Additionally, accounting for the practical dynamic environments for the UEs, we design an adaptive re-allocation strategy to enhance the system resilience. Extensive experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of AC$^2$P$^2$SL in reducing training time while ensuring data privacy preservation.

</details>


### [69] [An Empirical Analysis of High-Performance Computing Education in Germany](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31300)
*Anna-Lena Roth,Jonas Posner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 基于对德国102所高校的实证评估，发现67.6%的院校提供HPC课程但多集中在硕士选修阶段，61.8%的院校拥有HPC集群但仅23.0%明确用于教学；教学访问受限与课程中实践能力培养不足存在显著关联，揭示了德国高等教育中HPC理论与实践教学之间的结构性失衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC教育的重要性日益增长，但缺乏关于HPC教育如何实施以及课程与本地基础设施之间关联的跨机构实证证据。

Method: 对德国102所学术机构进行系统实证评估，基于模块手册和课程目录识别178门HPC相关课程，评估其能力覆盖和课程设置；同时评估本地HPC集群基础设施的可获得性、规模和教学可访问性，并进行统计分析。

Result: 67.6%的机构提供至少一门HPC课程，但多为硕士选修；61.8%拥有HPC集群，仅23.0%明确记录可用于教学；教学访问受限与课程中资源管理、集群使用、并行调试和性能分析等实践能力培养不足显著相关。

Conclusion: 德国高等教育中HPC教育存在结构性失衡，理论教学与实践能力培养之间脱节，教学基础设施的可访问性是制约实践能力发展的关键因素。

Abstract: The growing importance of High-Performance Computing (HPC) requires the systematic integration of parallel programming and performance-oriented competencies into computational science curricula. Effective HPC education combines theoretical foundations with practical experience on real cluster infrastructures, enabling students to understand scalability, efficiency, and architectural differences between shared and distributed memory systems. However, cross-institutional evidence on how HPC education is implemented, and how curricula relate to locally available infrastructure, remains limited. We address this gap through a systematic empirical assessment of HPC education at 102 academic institutions in Germany. Based on module handbooks and course catalogs, we identified 178 HPC-related courses and evaluated their competency coverage and curricular placement. We additionally assessed local academic HPC cluster infrastructures with respect to availability, size, and documented accessibility for teaching. The results show that 67.6% of institutions offer at least one HPC-related course, but these offerings are predominantly elective modules at the master's level, with limited integration in bachelor's programs. Although 61.8% of institutions operate HPC clusters, only 23.0% explicitly document their availability for educational use, as infrastructures are mainly reserved for research. Statistical analysis indicates a significant association between restricted teaching access and reduced curricular emphasis on practical competencies such as resource management, cluster usage, parallel debugging, and performance analysis. Overall, the findings reveal a structural imbalance between theoretical instruction and the development of practical HPC competencies in German higher education.

</details>


### [70] [Performance Analysis in Parallel Programming Education: A Comparative Usability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31458)
*Anna-Lena Roth,David James,Jonas Posner,Michael Kuhn*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EduMPI是一个学习支持工具，通过简化集群使用和提供直观的MPI通信可视化，帮助学生降低并行编程和性能分析的学习门槛。用户研究表明，与专业工具相比，EduMPI能有效提升教育价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有性能分析工具复杂度高，需要掌握集群架构、资源管理、MPI及常见并行问题等知识，难以融入教学环境。为克服这些障碍，开发了EduMPI学习支持工具。

Method: 开发了EduMPI工具，提供直观的GUI来自动化程序在集群上的执行，并实时可视化MPI通信过程，使学生能够根据进程在集群中的物理位置追踪通信并交互式检测性能问题。通过用户研究将EduMPI与专业性能分析工具进行对比。

Result: EduMPI降低了并行编程入门门槛，帮助学生直观理解并行程序性能，相比专业工具具有更高的教育价值。

Conclusion: EduMPI作为一种教育工具，能有效促进学生对MPI并行编程和性能分析的理解，适合用于并行编程课程教学。

Abstract: Parallel programming curricula encompass not only the development of parallel code and algorithm design but also emphasize efficiency, optimization, and performance analysis. To equip students with the skills necessary for writing efficient parallel code using message passing with MPI, practical experience on HPC environments is essential. Performance analysis tools assist in identifying issues such as load imbalances or bottlenecks. Despite their use by experienced developers, these tools' complexity and required knowledge of cluster architectures, resource management, MPI, and common parallel issues hinder their educational integration. To address these barriers, we developed EduMPI, a learning support tool designed to simplify cluster usage and performance analysis for students. EduMPI offers an intuitive GUI that automates program execution on clusters and delivers near-real-time visualizations of MPI communication. This enables students to track process communication according to their physical placement within the cluster and detect performance problems interactively. This paper presents a user study comparing EduMPI with established professional performance analysis tools, demonstrating that EduMPI lowers entry barriers and fosters an intuitive understanding of parallel program performance, thereby enhancing its educational value.

</details>


### [71] [LASER: Load-Aware Serving with Early-Exit for Reasoning LLMs at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31580)
*Zhiqing Tang,Size Li,Hanshuai Cui,Zilan Huang,Jianxiong Guo,Tian Wang,Yuan Wu,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LASER提出了一种面向边缘部署的负载感知早期退出服务框架，通过动态调整置信度阈值和推理预算分配，在推理质量几乎不损失（平均准确率仅降1%）的情况下，将平均延迟降低17-38%，SLO满意度提升3-6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（如DeepSeek-R1）在边缘设备部署时面临长思维链序列与资源受限之间的矛盾，现有的基于置信度的早期退出方法采用固定阈值，忽略了多请求并发和负载波动问题。

Method: (1) 负载感知的自适应退出阈值：根据实时系统负载在经验验证的鲁棒范围内动态调整置信度阈值；(2) 难度与负载感知的推理预算预分配：根据请求难度和系统容量分配计算资源。将问题建模为推理质量与服务延迟的联合优化。

Result: 在两个推理模型、四个基准测试和多种负载条件下，相比固定阈值基线，平均延迟降低17-38%，SLO满意度提升3-6%，平均准确率仅下降1%。

Conclusion: LASER通过负载感知的早期退出策略有效解决边缘场景下长推理序列的延迟问题，在几乎不牺牲准确率的前提下显著提升服务质量和资源利用率。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) such as DeepSeek-R1 have achieved strong performance through extended chain-of-thought (CoT) generation. However, deploying them on edge devices raises a conflict between long CoT sequences and constrained resources. Recent confidence-based early exit methods reduce CoT length for individual requests, yet they apply fixed thresholds from a single-request perspective, ignoring multi-request concurrency and load fluctuation in edge serving. To bridge this gap, we propose \underline{L}oad-\underline{A}ware \underline{S}erving with \underline{E}arly-exit for \underline{R}easoning (LASER). LASER couples two complementary designs: (1) a load-aware adaptive exit threshold that adjusts the confidence bar based on real-time system load within an empirically validated robust range, and (2) a difficulty- and load-aware reasoning budget pre-allocation that assigns compute resources by request difficulty and system capacity. We formulate the problem as a joint optimization of reasoning quality and service latency. Experiments on two reasoning models, four benchmarks, and diverse load conditions show that LASER reduces average latency by 17--38\% and improves service-level objective (SLO) satisfaction by 3--6\% over fixed-threshold baselines, at an average accuracy cost of only 1\%.

</details>


### [72] [Decoupling Trust in Byzantine CRDTs: Fine-grained Post-Compromise Handling without Breaking Causality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31759)
*Amos Brocco*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将身份信任与内容信任解耦的细粒度信任模型，通过确定性重建允许副本保留已接受的更新，同时基于来源身份和更新语义选择性包含或排除更新，从而在拜占庭设置下实现CRDT的鲁棒一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CRDT假设良性参与者，在拜占庭或后妥协场景中，基于身份过滤的收敛方法难以处理此前正确节点变恶意后的更新回溯排除问题，可能破坏因果依赖关系。

Method: 将身份信任与内容信任解耦，建立同时包含两个维度的细粒度信任模型；基于确定性重建机制，允许根据发起者身份（如公钥）和个体更新的语义进行选择性包含或排除，并支持应用层策略。

Result: 所提方法能够保留先前已接受的更新，同时支持基于身份和内容的精细选择，保持因果一致性，在拜占庭和故障场景下实现鲁棒灵活的处理。

Conclusion: 该工作为去中心化CRDT系统中的拜占庭行为和故障行为提供了鲁棒且灵活的解决方案，在保持因果一致性的同时实现了更细粒度的信任控制。

Abstract: Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs) provide strong eventual consistency without coordination, but classical approaches assume benign participants. In Byzantine settings, convergence is typically enforced through agreement on update validity, often relying on identity-based filtering. However, such approaches struggle in post-compromise scenarios, where a previously correct participant becomes malicious: retroactive exclusion of its updates may break causal dependencies and invalidate subsequent computations. In this paper, we decouple identity-based trust from content-based trust and introduce a fine-grained trust model that combines both dimensions. Building on deterministic reconstruction, our approach allows replicas to preserve previously accepted updates while enabling selective inclusion or exclusion based on both the originating identity (e.g., public keys) and the semantics of individual updates. Trust decisions can incorporate application-level policies, enabling precise control over the impact of each update on the system state. Our approach preserves causal consistency and enables robust and flexible handling of both Byzantine and faulty behavior in decentralized CRDT systems.

</details>

### [73] [LUMOS: A Semantic Operating-System Layer for Accessibility-Grounded AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30697)
*Yogeswar Reddy Thota*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: LUMOS 是一个语义交互层，通过将操作系统可访问性元数据和浏览器UI结构转换为机器可读的语义蓝图，使AI智能体能够绕过截图和OCR的低效方式，直接利用结构化语义信息与操作系统交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作系统接口为人类用户优化（像素、图标、窗口、鼠标等），但AI智能体需要紧凑的语义状态、可落地操作和可靠反馈。当前智能体被迫使用截图、OCR和视觉裁剪，导致高Token成本、视觉歧义、延迟和坐标不确定性。

Method: LUMOS 将原生可访问性元数据和浏览器UI结构转换为机器可读的语义蓝图（包含稳定标识符、角色、名称、值、边界和操作能力），并通过操作系统自动化API支持实时语义指针定位，LLM通过可访问性基础的观察-行动循环使用受限的可见UI原语进行操作。

Result: LUMOS 减少了智能体对截图的依赖，利用操作系统已提供的语义结构实现更高效的交互。

Conclusion: LUMOS 不主张替代视觉智能体，而是减少对截图的依赖，为AI原生操作系统和机器可读交互层的发展提供了方向。

Abstract: Current operating systems expose interfaces optimized for human users but not for AI agents. Humans benefit from pixels, icons, windows, visual grouping, mouse movement, and keyboard shortcuts; AI agents instead need compact semantic state, grounded actions, and reliable feedback. As a result, many computer-use agents are forced to interpret screenshots, OCR output, and visual crops, introducing high token costs, visual ambiguity, latency, and coordinate uncertainty. This paper introduces LUMOS (Language Model Unified Machine-Readable Operating-System Semantics), a semantic interaction layer between AI agents and operating systems. LUMOS converts native accessibility metadata and browser UI structures into machine readable semantic blueprints with stable identifiers, roles, names, values, bounds, and action affordances. It also supports live semantic pointer grounding by querying the UI element under or near the cursor through operating-system automation APIs. An LLM then acts through an accessibility grounded observe act loop using constrained visible-UI primitives rather than application-specific scripts. LUMOS does not claim to replace visual agents; instead, it reduces dependence on screenshots when operating systems already provide semantic structure. These results suggest a path toward AI-native operating systems and machine-readable interaction layers.

</details>


### [74] [Ensuring Deterministic Timing in a Federated GNSS Correction Pipeline with Lingua Franca](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31415)
*Tejeswini Jayaramareddy,Hoeseok Yang,Hokeun Kim*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文通过联邦式Lingua Franca程序实现GNSS校正数据管道，展示了嵌入式系统在显式逻辑时间语义下可具备确定性时序结构，并通过实验验证了中断节奏、环形缓存演化、分包行为及物理-逻辑抖动的可预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"嵌入式系统因硬件中断、缓冲和分布式通信而本质上异步且难以分析"的传统认知，证明其在显式逻辑时间建模下可呈现确定性时序结构。

Method: 将GNSS校正数据管道实现为联邦式Lingua Franca应用，将端到端管道分解为具有显式时间语义的反应器（reactors），包括：时间触发的GNSS接收器、基于波特率和FIFO缓冲区特性导出的UART中断流、周期性转发任务以及带抖动监控的下游处理。

Result: 联邦执行和运行时日志验证了理论推导的确定性时序结构，包括中断节奏、环形缓冲区演化、分包行为以及物理-逻辑抖动，获得了可复现且可预测的时序特征。

Conclusion: 基于显式逻辑时间语义的LF模型能够为通常被视为异步的嵌入式系统提供确定性的时序分析框架，保障系统的可预测性和可复现性。

Abstract: Embedded systems that combine hardware interrupts, buffering, and distributed communication are often perceived as inherently asynchronous and difficult to analyze. However, such systems can exhibit a deterministic timing structure when modeled using explicit logical-time semantics.
  This paper presents a Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) correction-data pipeline implemented as a federated Lingua Franca (LF) application. The federated LF program decomposes the end-to-end pipeline into reactors with explicit time semantics, including a time-triggered GNSS receiver, a UART interrupt stream derived from baud rate and First-In First-Out (FIFO) buffer characteristics, a periodic forwarding task, and downstream processing with jitter monitoring.
  Federated execution and runtime logs validate the analytically derived deterministic timing structure-including interrupt cadence, ring-buffer evolution, packetization behavior, and physical--logical jitter-yielding a reproducible and predictable timing profile.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [What Drives Interactive Improvement from Feedback?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30774)
*Bartłomiej Cupiał,Jan Łojek,Mikołaj Garstecki,Szymon Pobłocki,Alicja Ziarko,Piotr Miłoś*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过受控的学生-教师协议实验发现，多轮交互中自然语言反馈带来的提升往往被重复尝试本身的增益所混淆。自生成反馈几乎不优于无引导的自我修正，而外部强教师才能带来显著的反馈特定增益。交互提升更多取决于学生利用反馈的能力，而非教师身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为自然语言反馈能提升多轮语言智能体的表现，但高最终准确率可能来自重采样、格式修正或额外测试时计算，而非真正的反馈利用。作者希望区分"反馈带来的真实提升"与"重复尝试本身带来的增益"。

Method: 提出受控的学生-教师协议，在Omni-MATH、Codeforces、BBEH Linguini、ARC-AGI1四个基准上，评估13个开源模型作为学生和教师。比较外部反馈、自反馈和无引导自我修正三种条件，并控制交互历史、任务难度和教师对特权信息的访问。

Result: 多轮提升通常不能证明反馈被有效利用；自生成反馈比无引导自我修正增益极小；最强外部教师能产生显著更大的反馈特定增益；密集的学生-教师交互矩阵表明，交互增益更多由学生利用反馈的能力驱动，而非教师身份，但对固定学生而言教师选择仍然重要。

Conclusion: 基于反馈的智能体应在重复尝试基线之上进行评估；利用反馈行动的能力（而非仅仅反馈的可获得性）是交互式提升的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: We study when natural-language feedback produces improvement beyond the gains obtainable from repeated attempts alone. In multi-turn language agent setting, higher final accuracy can reflect useful feedback, but it can also arise from resampling, format correction, or additional test-time computation. To separate these effects, we introduce a controlled student-teacher protocol across Omni-MATH, Codeforces, BBEH Linguini, and ARC-AGI1, evaluating thirteen open-weight models in both student and teacher roles. We compare external feedback, self-feedback, and unguided self-refinement, while varying interaction history, task difficulty, and teacher access to privileged task information. Across settings, we find that multi-turn improvement is often not evidence of feedback use: self-generated feedback adds little beyond unguided self-refinement, whereas the strongest external teachers produce substantially larger feedback-specific gains, suggesting that useful feedback must provide guidance beyond generic retry. Dense student-teacher interaction matrices further show that interactive gains are driven more by the student's ability to use feedback than by the teacher's identity, although teacher choice remains important for a fixed student. These results suggest that feedback-based agents should be evaluated against repeated-attempt baselines, and that ability to act on feedback, not merely feedback availability, is a central bottleneck for interactive improvement. We release our controlled student-teacher evaluation framework at https://j-lojek.github.io/feedback-generation-is-a-bottleneck/.

</details>


### [76] [Contrastive Reflection for Iterative Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30840)
*Derek Koh,Jinghui Mo,Benjamin H. Le,Jiening Zhan,Baofen Zheng,Kevin Bevis,Nathaniel C. Owen,Lauren Elizabeth Charney,Wenqiong Liu,Jingwei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对比反思（Contrastive Reflection），一种针对智能体信息检索工作流的迭代式提示优化框架。通过识别错误锚定的行为切片，添加邻近成功示例，并让教师LLM提出针对性提示修改，在HotpotQA上将有监督精确匹配准确率从51.4%提升至60.4%，优于失败仅和随机证据变体，接近MIPROv2（59.4%）和GEPA（57.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在信息检索中扮演核心角色（检索、合成回答、评估），但控制智能体的提示优化在应用场景中更像调试而非盲目搜索。工程师需要知道哪个行为失败了、哪些邻近行为仍然有效、两者的区别，以及提示编辑能否在不引入回归的情况下提升保留集质量。现有方法缺乏这种可解释的对比调试机制。

Method: 提出对比反思框架：1）基于任务中心的质量定义，QA智能体暴露检索或推理轨迹，评分智能体暴露维度级分数和理由；2）识别错误锚定的行为切片，添加同一区域的邻近成功示例；3）让教师LLM提出针对性提示编辑；4）仅在验证性能提升时接受候选编辑，可选进行回归检查。框架使用树形切片选择器进行实例化。

Result: 在HotpotQA检索增强QA设置上，一次树选择的对比修复将保留集精确匹配准确率从51.4%提升至60.4%。失败仅和随机证据变体提升较少且破坏了更多先前正确的示例。与轻量指令对比：MIPROv2达59.4%，GEPA达57.0%。

Conclusion: 对比反思是一种可解释的、验证驱动的提示优化循环，适用于IR智能体，使提示修复更具可检查性，并显著优于失败仅和随机证据基线，性能接近现代提示优化器。

Abstract: LLM agents are becoming central to information retrieval: they issue retrieval queries, synthesize answers, and increasingly serve as judges for IR evaluation. Improving the prompts that control these agents is an optimization problem, but in applied IR settings it often looks less like blind search and more like debugging. Engineers need to know which behavior failed, which nearby behavior still worked, what distinguishes the two, and whether a prompt edit improves held-out quality without introducing regressions.
  We present Contrastive Reflection, an iterative prompt-optimization framework for agentic IR workflows. The framework starts from a task-centric quality definition: QA agents expose retrieval or reasoning traces, and grading agents expose dimension-level scores and rationales. These structured traces are used to identify error-anchored behavioral slices, add nearby successful examples from the same region, and ask a Teacher LLM to propose a targeted prompt edit. Candidate edits are accepted only when validation performance improves, optionally subject to regression checks. We instantiate the framework with a tree-based slice selector, but the contribution is the contrastive reflection loop rather than the tree itself.
  On a public HotpotQA retrieval-augmented QA setup, one tree-selected contrastive repair improves held-out exact-match accuracy from 51.4% to 60.4%. Failure-only and random-evidence variants improve less and break more previously correct examples. A light instruction-only comparison places the method near modern prompt optimizers: MIPROv2 reaches 59.4% and GEPA 57.0%. The result is an interpretable optimization loop for IR agents, aimed at making prompt repair more inspectable and validation-driven.

</details>


### [77] [How Can AI Find My Model? A Model-Finding Experimental Study Considering Data Formats, Embeddings, and Retrieval Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30846)
*Jhon G. Botello,Jose J. Padilla,Erika Frydenlund,Krzysztof Rechowicz,Eric Weisel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了数据表示、嵌入模型和检索策略对基于自然语言查询的仿真模型发现性能的影响，发现数据表示方式至关重要，开源嵌入模型能达到高性能，重排序方法在复杂查询中尤为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真模型复用中，如何在众多共存模型中识别出与建模意图相符的模型仍然是一个基础性挑战，现有方法难以在语义层面有效运作。

Method: 通过实验研究评估数据表示、基于Transformer的嵌入模型和检索策略对仿真模型发现的影响，使用多种查询类型，以recall@5和nDCG@5作为评价指标。

Result: 数据表示方式对性能有显著影响；开源嵌入模型可以达到高性能；重排序方法的重要性随查询复杂度增加而提升。

Conclusion: 该工作为AI驱动的模型发现提供了基线，并讨论了其在推动AI驱动的可组合性和互操作性方面的作用。

Abstract: Discovering simulation models for reuse remains a fundamental challenge in Modeling and Simulation (M&S). When many models coexist, identifying those that align with a given modeling intent remains difficult. Recent advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI), particularly retrieval-based approaches, offer a promising pathway to operate at this semantic layer. In this paper, we present an experimental study investigating the impact of data representation, transformer-based embedding models, and retrieval strategies on the discovery of simulation models using natural language queries. We evaluated performance across multiple query types using standard information retrieval metrics, including recall@5 and nDCG@5. Results show that data representation matters, open-source embedding models can achieve high performance, and reranking methods are important, especially as query complexity increases. This work provides a baseline for AI-driven model discovery and discusses its role in advancing toward AI-driven composability and interoperability.

</details>


### [78] [BayesBench: Evaluating LLM Belief Trajectories Under Multi-Turn Evidence Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30850)
*Ankur Samanta,Akshayaa Magesh,Tal Lancewicki,Ayush Jain,Youliang Yu,Paul Sajda,Kaveh Hassani,Aditya Modi,Daniel R. Jiang,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.AI
